Company News · Model reference guide

Honghua 3NB-1600HL Mud Pump Spare Parts

Published by Pratt Oil · 21 October 2026

The Honghua 3NB-1600HL list includes fluid-end assembly HTNB161.00 for the NB106/HTNB161 configuration. Keep that arrangement distinct from NB1602 entries when identifying cylinder, valve and liner components for replacement. For a quotation, send the part or drawing number, quantity, pump model and nameplate photo.

Technical details and source references

Confirm the installed model, drawing revision and dimensions before ordering. Catalogue quantities do not indicate current stock.

Reference documents and scope

  • S1 — Honghua 3NB-1600HL catalogue, HTNB900+HTNB161-TC, November 2018. Reference file: 宏华3NB-1600HL 2018钻井泵产品图册.pdf.
  • S2 — Honghua 3NB-1600HL catalogue, NB1602-TC, August 2019, Russian. Reference file: 俄语 3NB-1600HL.pdf.
  • S3 — Honghua 3NB-1600HL catalogue, NB1602-TC, November 2011, Chinese/Russian. Reference file: 宏华HHF1600HL（NB1602）.PDF.

PDF page numbers count from the first page of each reference file. Brand names identify the equipment or catalogue source. A listed number does not establish interchangeability, original-manufacturer authorisation, stock or a delivery commitment. Send the nameplate, complete reference, quantity and drawing revision for a parts review.

Ordering Honghua 3NB-1600HL spare parts starts with identifying the catalogue configuration. The model name appears on several parts books whose power-end and fluid-end drawing families differ. A matching pump name alone does not establish that a liner, valve component, stud or seal will fit.

This guide distinguishes the NB1602 and NB106/HTNB161 catalogue configurations and explains which identifiers to include in a parts enquiry.

Identify the parts-book version before selecting a reference

Catalogue identificationPower-end referencesFluid-end referenceIdentification point
NB1602-TC, November 2011, Chinese/RussianGH3161-01.00(G) frame; GH3161-02.00(G) crankshaftNB1602.01.00Cover names the pump 3NB-1600HL.
NB1602-TC, August 2019, RussianGH3161-series power-end assembliesNB1602.01.00Valve, piston and liner references must follow the 2019 table, rather than being carried over from the older book.
HTNB900+HTNB161-TC, November 2018NB106.01.00 frame; NB106.02.00 crankshaftHTNB161.00Lists a separate ten-digit material Code alongside the drawing number.

Keep the cover identification and the relevant assembly drawing together. A GH3161 reference and an NB106 reference are not interchangeable identifiers simply because both books say 3NB-1600HL.

Liners and pistons: keep the size designation with the drawing number

The 2019 book lists the bimetallic-liner family HTNB162.04.00 and piston family HTNB506.02.00. Its liner table covers size designations 120, 130, 140, 150, 160, 170 and 180; its piston table starts at 130. The 2018 book instead uses HTNB161.05.00 for the bimetallic-liner family and HTNB161.07.00 for the piston family.

Version and componentDrawing numberMaterial codeSize designation printed in the table
2019 bimetallic linerHTNB162.04.021200538980120
2019 bimetallic linerHTNB162.04.061200538973160
2019 pistonHTNB506.02.01.001200576187∅130
2018 bimetallic linerHTNB161.05.011200535433120

These values are source size designations, not a conversion table. Confirm the units, liner bore, piston construction and seal arrangement against the actual component drawing. Do not extend a listed part number to a size that the table does not list.

Use both the material code and the drawing number

In the 2018 parts book, material code 1200535177 is associated with fluid-end assembly drawing HTNB161.00. Material code 1200464444 identifies the source row for crosshead assembly NB106.04.00. Supplying both fields preserves the relationship between the purchasing code and the engineering drawing.

The reference tables distinguish custom drawing numbers, material codes, bought-in component designations and standards. A GB/T standard describes a standardised item family; it is not a unique Honghua spare-part number. Include the fastener size or other listed specification with it.

What to send for a 3NB-1600HL parts enquiry

  • Pump nameplate photograph and serial number.
  • Parts-book cover or revision, plus the relevant assembly page.
  • Exact drawing number and material code where available.
  • Item number, quantity and the installed part photograph.
  • For liners and pistons: size designation, measured bore, seal details and any construction requirement.
  • For studs and nuts: both thread ends, length and the thread form shown on the drawing.

Browse our mud pump spare-parts supply information and include the catalogue version when requesting a quotation. Supply and suitability should be confirmed against the submitted component details.
